市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8
美國零售柴油價格與通膨預期走勢對比
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

顧
美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響
資料來源：美國銀行，2026.7
資料來源：復華投信，2026.9

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

科技產業AI資本支出規模最大企業
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.23

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本

## Document 073: 261005_daiwa_TW-CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_daiwa_TW-CCL.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-05

### Page 1

Summary: After market hours on 5 October 2026, EMC, TUC, and ITEQ released their 
monthly sales results. Our read-through is positive as the three key CCL vendors beat 
sales expectations by 5-15% on better pricing and on-going spec upgrades. Our thesis is 
that the strong AI demand is causing a spill-over impact on the whole CCL industry with 
low-end CCL having c.100% price hike in 2026E and high-end CCL seeing a 40-50% price 
hike due to cost inflation in 2026E. Recent data points showed this thesis remains valid. 
Looking ahead, we expect high-end/low-end CCL to see additional c.10%/20% ASP 
increases in 4Q26E, which could be the key share price catalyst. We believe that recent 
negatives news (including the impact of PTFE and CPO/NPO on the industry) are 
overdone. The impact on high-end CCL from these new technologies is likely to be limited 
from the point of view of Taiwan CCL companies. As such, we remain positive that the 
three names in our CCL coverage will ride on the decent price hike trend given favourable 
supply-demand dynamics.

• EMC (2383 TT, TWD5,700, Buy [1]) 
EMC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD21.5bn (+7% MoM, +169% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD60.9bn; +29% QoQ, +142% YoY) reaching 112% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD54.4bn) and 110% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD55.5bn). We attribute 
the revenue strength to the higher-than-expected price hike and on-going spec upgrades 
(leading to higher ASPs). Management attributes c.50% of the MoM increase in revenue in 
September to the price hike and the other 50% came to product mix optimisation. Looking 
ahead, the company doesn’t rule out the potential to increase its ASP again in 4Q26 (after 
the first time in 2Q26) in order to reflect rising raw material prices since 2Q26. As such, we 
expect revenue to grow at least high teens again on the potential price head as well as 
ongoing spec upgrades.

EMC sees limited impact from PTFE and HC glass-fibre-free CCL. Based on our 
understanding, PTFE is still under certification accounting for only limited layers inside 
specified high-end PCB as of now. As such, its impact on the high-end CCL TAM should be 
limited. As for HC glass-fibre-free CCL, the rigidity of PCB using this type of CCL could be 
a key concern to us.

Lastly, regarding the recent Co-Packaged Optics/Near-Packaged Optics (CPO/NPO) 
news’s negative impact on the CCL TAM, we also believe it to be limited given the likely 
adoption date for CPO of 2030, based on EMC’s forecast. Besides, we believe the PCB 
board inside CPO scale-up switches at that time would still need to support an 800G/1.6T 
transmission speed (or even lower) which would still require high-end PCB transmission 
although the highest transmission bandwidth can increase to 3.2T via optics. Lastly, EMC 
believes the spec of CCL won’t downgrade to M4 in the worst-case scenario but would be 
at least M6. Last but not least, we believe this kind of CPO-connected AI server might only

Given its leading position in the CCL industry and the likely imbalance between supply and 
demand in 2027, we remain positive view on EMC. We have a Buy (1) rating on EMC, with 
a 12-month TP of TWD7,200, based on a target PER of 38x applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected AI server demand.

• TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,780, Buy [1]) 
TUC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD6.1bn (+4% MoM, +115% YoY), which led to its 
3Q26 sales (TWD17.9bn; +26% QoQ, +123% YoY) reaching 103% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD17.4bn) and 105% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD17.1bn). Looking 
ahead, we expect TUC to deliver at least QoQ growth in 4Q26 given 1) a price hike along 
with its peers for high-end CCL (eg. M8) and 2) some shipment delay from 3Q26 given 
flooding in Southeast Asia. For the longer term, the company is promising a continual spec 
upgrade trend in 2027 with new projects using M9-grade CCL (including low dk1, low dk2, 
and Q-glass version) in 2027 for high-end switches, AI servers, and optical transceivers. 
Considering the insufficient capacity in the CCL industry, we expect TUC to further optimise 
its product mix after acquiring more high-end CCL orders in future quarters (vs. a single 
major AI server client in 2026). We have a Buy (1) rating on TUC, with a 12-month TP of 
TWD2,320, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for switches/AI servers, and 2) market 
share loss to CCL peers.

• ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD751, Buy [1]) 
ITEQ’s September 2026 revenue was TWD5.8bn (+15% MoM, +111% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD15.6bn; +35% QoQ, +102% YoY) reaching 114% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD13.7bn) and 108% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD14.5bn). The number 
for 3Q26 was higher than our estimate due likely to a stronger-than-expected price hike 
(c.30% in 3Q26 vs. expectation of 20%). Looking ahead to 4Q26, we expect the company 
to further raise its pricing by 20-30% to factor in cost inflation in raw materials (E-glass) 
and the supply-demand gap. The company also expects the rising supply of e-glass (which 
could lift its utilisation rate from the current level of 75%) to lift its gross margin more 
significantly. For 2027, the company is excited about the new CPU adoption driving rising 
M7 CCL usage and is working on M9 certification with new end customers, which could 
become a key growth driver in 2028. We have a Buy (1) rating on ITEQ. Key downside 
risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

## Document 105: 7856漢測_20261008_UBS

Source PDF: all report pdf/7856漢測_20261008_UBS.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 27
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 8 October 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Hermes Testing Solutions
Shifting-left, scaling-up: dual catalysts in chip
probing and CPO
Jack-of-all-trades for bespoke testing solutions in the semiconductor industry
We initiate coverage of Hermes Testing (HTSI) with a Buy rating and NT$8,880 PT. HTSI is
uniquely positioned to benefit from two key semiconductor testing trends: shift-left
testing and the emerging CPO testing market. Leveraging its long-standing relationship

with TSMC, HTSI has established capabilities across electrical test engineering,
customised test equipment, thermal control modules, probe cards, and CPO testing
solutions. We forecast revenue growth of 127%/152%/78% in 2026E/27E/28E, driven
by increasing adoption of thermal control modules for wafer-level test and initial
deployment of CPO insertion 2 and insertion 3 test equipment through ficonTEC.
Additional 2028E growth drivers include reflow tools, laser clean solutions, and MEMS

probe cards. The stock trades at 27/15x 2027/28E PE, versus ~35x NTM PE for handler-
equipment peers, offering re-rating potential as sales scale, new products are qualified,
and its CPO presence expands. See our companion testing deep dive sector note.
Shift-left beneficiary with exceptional thermal control modules
Thermal control modules should be HTSI's largest growth driver over the next several
years. We expect increasing chip power consumption, rising package complexity, and

tighter probe pitches to drive adoption of advanced thermal control solutions used
alongside leading-edge probers, in additional to more wafer level insertions and longer
test time. We estimate HTSI will ship approximately 3,000 thermal-control modules in
2027E, up from around 1,200 units in 2026E, sales contribution rise from ~30% in
Q226 to >50% of sales by 2027E. The launch of its second-generation platform in
2027E, which carries 25-50% higher ASPs, should further improve profitability. We

forecast GM expansion from 55.5% in 2026E to 58.3%/60.0% in 2027E/28E.
Unique position to capture CPO upside
Through its partnership with German optical-automation leader ficonTEC, HTSI is
positioned to participate in the initial ramp of CPO testing equipment. HTSI supports
local manufacturing, installation, automation and after-sales service, making it a key
enabler of volume production. We estimate HTSI captures approximately 30% of
equipment value across the 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems we forecast for

delivery in 2027E, with further potential upside in 2028E as adoption expands.
Valuation: initiate coverage with a Buy rating and price target NT$8,880
We forecast EPS of NT$29.1 in 2026E (including a one-off employee share purchase
programme impact), rising to NT$153.3/286.5 in 2027E/28E. Our NT$8,880 price target
is based on 31x average 2028E PE, versus handler-equipment peers forward PE at
roughly 35x, reflecting HTSI's unique exposure to both shift-left testing, CPO, and our

estimate of 32% LT earnings CAGR. We initiate with a Buy rating.

Hermes Testing Solutions UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will HTSI be able to keep its moat within chip probing thermal control modules?
Yes. We expect HTSI's competitive position to strengthen as chip power consumption, package
complexity, and probe density continue to increase. The company is developing next-generation
thermal control solutions, including liquid cooling technologies, while also expanding into specialized

chuck solutions designed to address wafer warpage. In addition, HTSI's close collaboration with
TSMC, TEL, Advantest, and other OSATs should reinforce its position within advanced wafer-level
testing.
Q: Will insertion 2 of CPO testing process be skipped?
No. We believe concerns surrounding insertion 2 adoption are overdone. While some industry
participants favor bypassing insertion 2 due to throughput and ROI considerations, doing so increases
the risk of defective optical engines being identified only after the components are delivered to

OSATs, creating yield accountability challenges and higher packaging losses. Based on our industry
studies, we expect insertion 2 testing to remain an important part of the CPO workflow over at least
the next two years as the industry focuses on improving PIC yields and overall testing efficiency.
We initiate coverage of Hermes Testing (HTSI) with a Buy rating and NT$8,880 price target. HTSI
offers unique exposure to two of the semiconductor testing industry's most attractive growth drivers:

1) shift-left testing and 2) CPO testing. The industry's shift-left testing strategy will have more test
insertions move to the wafer level before packaging, in addition to longer CP time. Leveraging its
longstanding relationship with TSMC, HTSI has established capabilities across thermal control
modules, electrical-test engineering services, customized equipment, and emerging CPO testing
solutions. We believe there could be re-rating potential as sales scale, new products are qualified, and

its CPO presence expands.
TSMC and OSATs have been expanding their CoWoS capacities aggressively, from 40kwpm in 2024
to around 90 kwpm in 2025, and is tracking to expand CoWoS capacity toward 160kwpm in 2026E.
TSMC also ramped up expanding N3/N2 capacity, with multiple product launches from customers'
pipelines—Nvidia's Rubin, AMD's MI400 series/Venice CPU and other cloud ASICs—translating into
foreseeable, strong testing demand.
Despite a 264% YTD share-price increase, HTSI currently trades at just 15x of our 2028E EPS versus

handler-equipment peers forward PE at roughly 35x. We believe market expectations for 2027 CPO
testing equipment shipments remain conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on
our industry analysis, we estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E,
this should be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3 shipment
due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands. Our

reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price implies only a 17% long-term sales CAGR
vs our estimate of 27% and long-term GM level of 33% vs our estimate of 61%. We see scope for
re-rating.

Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It focuses on providing
semiconductor testing and inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical support, and
maintenance services. Its main business segments are customized semiconductor equipment,

engineering services, and probe cards and other materials.

Hermes Testing Solutions UBS Research
Testing TAM expanding with IC complexity: leaders likely to
outperform industry growth
Our central view is that testing content per AI accelerator is growing faster than
semiconductor unit volumes, creating a multi-year expansion in testing TAM. We believe
semiconductor testing is entering a period of structural growth as AI-driven chip
complexity increases testing intensity across wafer, package and system levels. The

industry's shift-left strategy, which moves more testing upstream in the semiconductor
manufacturing process, is resulting in longer and more frequent test insertions. As cloud
AI GPUs and ASICs become increasingly complex, additional testing is required to ensure
functionality, performance and yield, while reducing defective parts per million (DPPM).
More robust testing should improve deployment efficiency and lower the cost of
operating AI infrastructure.

Semiconductor testing generally comprises four stages: wafer/chip probing (CP), final
test (FT), system-level test (SLT), and burn-in. CP is conducted at the wafer-level before
packaging, while FT, SLT, and burn-in occur after packaging. The below Figure 3S e m i c o n d u c t o r  t e s t i n g  p r o c e s s :  f r o m  w a f e r  l e v e l  t o  p a c k a g e  l e v e l
illustrates the semiconductor manufacturing flow.

We estimate semiconductor testing historically accounts for ~1% of total
semiconductor revenue. Within testing, consumables such as probe cards, sockets and
interface boards represent ~10-15% of industry revenue. According to Yole, the probe
card, socket and interface board markets should grow from US$3.8bn, US$2.3bn and
US$1.7bn in 2026 to US$5.5bn, US$3.1bn and US$2.0bn by 2031, respectively.

Nonetheless, leaders within the testing sector will standout among competition, with
greater sales CAGR comparing to the industry.
We see HTSI's sales for the next few years to mainly be driven by 1) thermal control
module shipment and 2) CPO equipment shipment. In the past, thermal control

modules weren't necessary for chip probing, due to less TDP for the chips. However, as
new iterations of AI accelerators now got substantial power draw, this unlocks a brand
new TAM for HTSI to enter with their thermal control modules to pair with probers, in
order to prevent probe card damages.
 Robust thermal control module shipments.  We estimate HTSI to ship
3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2027E/28E, with continuous demand in
future years, bringing their 2026-31E semiconductor equipment sales CAGR to

48%.

### Page 4

 Thermal control module ASP expansion.  Gen 2 thermal control modules will
start to ship in 2027E which will drive tool ASP up around 25-50%.
 Demand supported by Advantest expansions. We believe the 3,000 thermal
control modules shipment forecast for 2027E is not far-fetched, as we read-across
from Advantest's announced capacity expansion plan (testers will be paired with
probers with a 1:1 ratio) with increasing capacity up to 5,000 testers within

FY2027/3 and they also announced a 10,000-unit target within CY2028.
Although the testers capacity expansion plan includes testers for final test, we do
see a good portion to be allocated to chip probing given shift-left testing trend
increasing both testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CPO is another brand new TAM for semiconductor testing.  As insertion 2
and 3 testing equipment demand starts to rise in 2027 with scale-out CPO

adoption, we see HTSI to ship 40/70 insertion 2 and 3 equipment in 2027E, with
this number growing in 2028E to 80/160 and higher in future years as CPO
proliferates, and driving 52% 2026-31E test equipment & engineering services
sales CAGR.
 CPO to see content increase. Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment may start to ship in
2027E, with higher throughput and tool ASP .
 Insertion 2 might scale down, but not happening anytime soon. Insertion 2
equipment shipment might come down in 2029E and beyond, as foundries scale-

up yield for photonic IC (PIC) in order to lessen the time consuming insertion 2
testing burden.

Robust growth drivers across semiconductor testing
We believe HTSI is set to benefit from several semiconductor testing growth drivers and
capture the AI induced, expanding testing TAM. We see HTSI is uniquely positioned as
the main beneficiary of two of the strongest growth drivers within semiconductor
testing: testing shift-left and CPO.
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing

### Page 5

The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging.
Figure 4: CP is set to grow substantially with shift-left, each packaging step set to be MORE costly than the prior step

RDL / RWAT testing Wafer-level CP (WLCP)
CoWChiplet (if use SoIC) CoW
CP (Die A)
CP (Die B)
Assembly Test (AST)
oS
Source: UBS
The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while
defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed

testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million
(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall

integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of
wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected by the industry to surge another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level

chip probe (WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and
higher power densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield
degradation, creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal-control
solutions.

Hermes Testing (HTSI) is a key beneficiary of this trend. Leveraging its long-standing
relationship with TSMC and expertise in semiconductor test solutions, HTSI supplies

advanced thermal control modules used alongside leading-edge probers (made by
Tokyo Electron), particularly for AI applications. These thermal control modules are
required for high TDP semiconductor wafer probe in order to maintain the temperature

### Page 6

of the chuck to avoid needle burns or probe card damages. We estimate HTSI will ship
approximately 3,000 thermal-management modules in 2027E, up from 1,200 in 2026E,
supporting sales growth of 127%/152% in 2026E/27E. HTSI is also the partner for
prober installation, prober maintenance and services. In addition, HTSI provides high-
end chuck processing services, an area that should benefit from increasing package sizes
and higher wafer warpage associated with advanced packaging.

Beyond thermal control modules, HTSI also supplies consumables and customised
solutions for wafer-level testing, including probe card cleaning sheets and laser-based
probe head cleaning systems. As probing intensity increases under the shift-left testing
model, demand for cleaning and maintenance solutions should rise alongside probe
card utilisation. Currently, cleaning sheets is around 5% of sales, but we believe it could
gain traction in 2027/28E and beyond. We see additional upside from future modular

laser cleaning systems that could be integrated directly with probers, further expanding
HTSI's content opportunity within advanced wafer-level testing.
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor testing
As interconnect bottlenecks increasingly shift from the memory wall to the copper wall,
we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) adoption is inevitable over the longer term. Testing
remains one of the key bottlenecks to CPO deployment, as optical validation differs

fundamentally from the electrical testing processes that have dominated semiconductor
manufacturing for decades. As a result, CPO is creating a new testing opportunity across
the semiconductor equipment and consumables ecosystem. We have written
extensively on CPO and opportunities that it brings to the semiconductor supply chain,
see our CPO deep dive , our thoughts on CPO timing , 2026 SiPhIA conference
takeaways, and SEMICON 2026 takeaways.
Various test equipment suppliers are scrambling to come up with ideal testing

equipment across the four insertions of CPO testing, with solutions to ensure both
optical and electrical signal integrity of the CPO solutions. The four insertions are:
 Insertion 1: Electronic IC (EIC)/Photonic IC (PIC) test.  Single-side wafer test
that has dedicated probe cards to test the fabricated EIC/PIC wafers.
 Insertion 2: Optical/Electrical test. Double sided wafer test, testing the hybrid
bonded (e.g. via TSMC's COUPE) EIC and PIC wafers by an opto-electrical test that

verifies the electrical signals on the top and optical signal on the bottom of the
hybrid bonded wafers.
 Insertion 3: Optical engine (OE) test.  The OE tests are done to ensure the
quality of the OEs and with FAU (Fiber Array Unit) attached, to ensure the signal
integrity of the OEs. The testing equipment needs to be able to handle 224G per
channel data rates, requiring high unit per hour (UPH) throughput, meticulous dirt
mitigation, and maintaining a near 100% contact rating on high-speed sockets.

 Insertion 4: ASIC final test.  This final test step is established post the OEs are
packaged together with the ASIC. The complete ASIC package (now with CPO) is
now tested for opto-electrical functionalities.
As it stands, insertion 2 and insertion 3 are the two areas where there are the most
debates in the industry. Insertion 3 suppliers such as Chroma has noted the trend of
“shift-to-middle” paradigm centered on insertion 3. In high-density scale-out switches

deploying up to 32 optical engines (e.g. Nvidia’s Spectrum-X architectures), any OE
defect results in package scrap at the CoWoS level, making 100% known good die
(KGD) validation at the singulated OE stage an absolute commercial requirement.

With its 25 years of precision test equipment expertise, ficonTEC is an early mover for
CPO test equipment and has key know-how for optical alignment, which is
quintessential for optical test. HTSI, through its partnership with ficonTEC, is set to be a
major beneficiary once CPO proliferates in 2027E-28E. As the partner of ficonTEC,
Hermes Testing is the key enabler that can help ficonTEC achieve HVM for its CPO
testing equipment. As of now, our view is that both insertion 2 and insertion 3 should be

required within the next two years, despite the difficulties surrounding double-sided
wafer probing (insertion 2), as the yield for OEs are still not high enough to avoid
defective dies. Finding out known-good OEs will be crucial before the FAU coupling
process during the latter stages of CPO packaging, to prevent impeding the final
package with multiple OEs attached to the switch ASIC (e.g. 32 OEs on Nvidia's
Spectrum-X). We estimate Hermes Testing will ship 40/70 units of insertion 2/3 testing

equipment in 2027E, with potential upside to the numbers as we view Hermes Testing
will likely assist with the ramp up for Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment in 2027E. Gen 2
insertion equipment will come with better throughput and ASP .

Beyond insertions 2 and 3, HTSI is also positioned to participate in insertion 1 testing
through customised prober solutions for photonic integrated circuit (PIC) testing. We
believe these solutions could eventually be packaged as licensable intellectual property,

creating an additional long-term growth opportunity as CPO adoption broadens. While
near-term testing growth is driven by AI accelerators, CPO represents the next major
expansion cycle for semiconductor testing. We believe HTSI is uniquely positioned to
participate across multiple test insertions, providing exposure to a potentially significant
source of incremental revenue beginning in 2027E.

HTSI's unique position for new TAM of thermal control
modules and CPO test equipment
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan, as a spin-off
from Hermes-Epitek. The company provides semiconductor testing and inspection

solutions, engineering services, customized equipment, and test consumables. HTSI
maintains a global service footprint across Taiwan (Hsinchu, Tainan), Mainland China
(Suzhou), Japan (Tokyo, Kumamoto), Singapore, Malaysia, and the United States
(Arizona), enabling close support for leading semiconductor customers.
Its main business segments are structured around three pillars:
 Semiconductor equipment and customized products : HTSI utilises a dual-

track strategy of independent R&D and also equipment agency sales. They offers

### Page 9

highly customized solutions designed to address specific client pain points and
bottlenecks. Key products include: thermal control modules that pair with a 1:1
ratio with wafer-level test probers, electron beam inspection equipment, white
light interference optical inspection systems, laser probe cleaning machines, and
the SYMPHONY fluxless reflow system.
 Testing equipment engineering services:  The company operates the largest
engineering service team in Taiwan, consisting of over 340 technical professionals.

The engineering service team provides extensive equipment life-cycle support,
including installation, relocations, modification, testing, routine maintenance, and
repair services. CPO related equipment sales/services will be recognized within this
segment, as CPO are not direct HTSI products, but rather sales recognition via its
partnership with ficonTEC.
 Probe card design, manufacturing, and cleaning materials:  HTSI delivers a
comprehensive product portfolio that supports the entire probe card life-cycle. For

probe cards, they supply membrane, cantilever, and vertical probe cards. As they
are small in scale, HTSI targets niche probe card applications such as RF, 5G/6G,
millimeter wave, LCD driver, low end logics, TIA (Transimpedance Amplifier), etc. In
addition to the three existing probe technologies, HTSI also collaborates with MJC
in Japan for MEMS probe development, targeting sales in 2028E for high-end, AI/
HPC related applications. Cleaning materials is another important business within

this segment, as HTSI source and process sand paper or other materials to offer
cleaning sheets to clean the probe cards, removing oxidations, unwanted particles
and substances that accumulated on the probe cards during the testing process.
The sales resiliency in the coming years mainly stems from its robust relationship with key
industry players, especially as an engineering and service provider with TSMC for testing
solutions. This sets up a solid foundation for its customized equipment business, as they

know the specific testing bottleneck to tackle. For instance, as Hermes-Epitek
manufacturers the probers for Tokyo Electron (TEL) and HTSI helps with installation,
services, and maintenance of the probers, they understand the thermal bottlenecks that
the wafer-level test industry has been facing and can effectively come up with module
solutions to solve this.
Margins set to expand with value-added products and services
The company has demonstrated strong execution in advanced wafer-level testing, with

H126 revenue of NT$2.2bn already exceeding 90% of FY25 revenue (NT$2.4bn).
Profitability has also improved significantly, with GM increasing from 42.1% in 2025 to
54.9% in H126.
With share of value-added products and services set to rise in 2027E in sales mix, HTSI is
set to see GM and operating leverage expansion. As a niche engineering service provider
that has robust ties to leading foundries and OSATs, we believe HTSI has substantial
pricing power and estimate HTSI' GM for thermal control modules to be around 65%,

with GM for CPO equipment of around 60% as HTSI provides an engineering-service-
like business for it. We believe these factors will help the company to reach a 60% long-
term GM range.
OPM is also set to expand with customized equipment sales rise in total sales mix. If we
exclude the one-time expense to be recognized during Q326 for employee stock
benefits (we estimate around NT$1bn), YoY opex growth will be around 99% for
2027E, with downside risk if the company conducts tight cost control measures. We

forecast the company's OPM will expand from 19.5% in 2026E, to 44.3%/47.0% in
2027/28E.

Source: LSEG, UBS
Despite a 264% YTD share-price increase, HTSI still trades at just 15x 2028E P/E. We
believe market expectations for 2027E CPO testing equipment shipments remain
conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on our industry analysis, we
estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, this should
be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3
shipment due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO

adoption expands. Our reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price
implies only a 17% long-term sales CAGR vs our estimate of 27% and long-term GM
level of 33% vs our estimate of 61%. We see scope for re-rating.
We believe the market is not fully reflecting three key growth drivers:
1) Shift-left testing is still underappreciated.
As advanced packaging costs rise and AI accelerators become increasingly complex,
more thorough testing is done at the wafer-level. This should drive sustained demand

for HTSI's thermal control modules, probe card consumables, and other wafer-level
testing solutions.
2) CPO revenue potential remains underestimated.
Investor expectations for 2027 CPO equipment shipments, particularly insertion 2
testing systems, remain conservative. Based on our industry studies, we forecast HTSI
will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, with further potential
upside in 2028E as CPO adoption broadens.
3) New products provide additional optionality.

Beyond its core businesses, HTSI is expanding into higher-value solutions including mass-
reflow tools, MEMS probe cards and laser cleaning systems, creating incremental
growth opportunities that we believe are largely absent from current expectations.
We value HTSI using 31x average 2028E PE, underpinned by a 32% 2027-31E earnings
CAGR. We use 2028E PE as our view is that 2028 is the year for thermal control modules
and CPO adoption grow from the initial ramping up stage to mass adoption stage. We

believe using handlers as a peer group is justifiable for a unique company like HTSI, with
thermal control modules for chip probing sharing similar characteristics as active thermal
control used for final test, in addition to Hon Precision and Chroma having exposure to
CPO test equipment. For equipment vendors, the peer group's average NTM PE post
2022 is around 35x, and the group is currently trading at around 41x NTM PE (see
What's Priced In chart).

HTSI is trading at NT$4,190 as of 6
Oct 2026

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is heavily skewed (2.2:1) to the upside.
UPSIDE (NT$10,000): Our upside scenario reflects stronger than expected thermal
module and CPO equipment shipment in 2027-28E, with additional upside from better
traction of probe cards and mass reflow tools, forming 157%/91% YoY sales growth in
2027/28E and even higher operating leverage with OPM of 46.3%/50.0% in 2027/28E.

This scenario implies a 34x 2028E PE, a upside driving by more equipment demand
stemming for robust shift-left test induced equipment bottleneck, alongside CPO
proliferation with scale-up CPO equipment pull-in in 2028E.
BASE (NT$8,880): Our price target of NT$8,880 is based on 31x 2028E PE. We expect an
earnings CAGR of 32% in 2027-31E. With shift-left trend for more thermal module
requirement during testing, alongside 40/70 CPO insertion 2/3 shipment. We see these

growth drivers driving YoY sales growth from a high base of 127% in 2026E to
152%/78% YoY in 2027/2028E, with OPM of 44.3%/47.0% in 2027/28E.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,500): Our downside scenario reflects lower shipment of thermal
control modules due to less advanced prober demand with less insertion 2 and 3
equipment shipment due to bottlenecks with CPO production, alongside limited
additional growth drivers to be picked up in 2028 such as MEMS probe card and mass

reflow tools. This leads to weaker YoY sales growth of 67%/50% in 2027/28E and
operating margin of 29.3%/32.0% in 2027/28E as equipment shipment fails to scale.
This scenario implies a 14x 2028E PE, as valuation de-rate with dimmed outlook without
CPO and limited upside from thermal control module shipments.

Company Description
Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu,
Taiwan. It focuses on providing semiconductor testing and
inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical support,
and maintenance services. Its main business segments are
customized semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and
probe cards and other materials.
Industry Outlook
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC

applications, are set to increase in both front-end wafer
fabrication advancements and back-end packaging complexities
with each iterations. With the industry now moves to utilize
"shift left", chip probing and its relevant supply chain will be key
beneficiaries of this trend which we believe will unlock multi-
year, secular growth trends.

Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It focuses on
providing semiconductor testing and inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical
support, and maintenance services. Its main business segments are customized
semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and probe cards and other materials.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Hermes Testing based on PE methodology.
Risks for the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in
the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development

such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech
companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected front-end/CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in
Hermes Testing gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from

either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in
Hermes Testing losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could
lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to
Hermes Testing, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of

the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact Hermes
Testing’s margins for equipment shipments.

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.

3 = no change, 5 = getting better, N/A = no view)

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 125: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서_9ee69886

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서_9ee69886.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

SK하이닉스 (000660)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 3,200,000원 
현재주가 (10.8) 1,681,000원 
상승여력 90.4%

## Document 126: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 소음보다 방향성에SK하이닉스(000660) 소음보다 방향성에소음보다 방향성에 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 소음보다 방향성에SK하이닉스(000660) 소음보다 방향성에소음보다 방향성에 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

기업분석 2025.09.28 www.daishin.com

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

## Document 194: A股9月复盘与10月展望：缩量调整中的结构性分化，与政策托底的十字路口

Source PDF: all report pdf/A股9月复盘与10月展望：缩量调整中的结构性分化，与政策托底的十字路口.pdf
Report source: 东吴证券
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 25
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观深度报告

东吴证券研究所

A 股 9 月复盘与 10 月展望——缩量调整中
的结构性分化，与政策托底的十字路口

2026 年 10 月 14 日

证券分析师  芦哲 
执业证书：S0600524110003 
 luzhe@dwzq.com.cn 
证券分析师  唐遥衎 
执业证书：S0600524120016 
 tangyk@dwzq.com.cn

相关研究  
《 9 月美国 CPI前瞻：核心环比预计
温和，仍需关注油价扰动——海外周
报 20261011》 
2026-10-11 
《9月 PPI同比增速或录得年内新高》 
2026-10-11

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 投资要点 
◼ 9 月行情的本质：海外估值调整下的结构性分化： 
◼ 2026 年 9 月万得全 A 回调 5.31%，呈“月初震荡、月中反抽、月末回
调”三段式，9 月 24 日、9 月 28 日出现的两次较明显调整，对整体走
势影响较大。美联储加息推升美债 10Y逼近 5%，全球无风险利率上行
压制大盘成长，外资流出、存量资金抱团低流动性微盘，大小盘剪刀差
走至分化。国内PMI尚稳，本轮回调更多来自估值端调整，业绩端暂未
明显走弱。10 月初延续弱势，10 月 8 日普跌、10 月 9 日深 V 放量修
复，但单日反转未确认趋势。 
◼ 行业分化：政策主线 vs周期回调： 
◼ 2026 年 9 月行业分化比大盘更明显。房地产+6.28%为 9 月领涨行业，
受房贷贴息+PSL 组合拳驱动；医药 +2.47%、银行+2.32%、美容护理
+0.80%体现防御、超跌与高股息。周期/成长线回调：有色-12.87%、电
力设备-9.46%、机械-8.53%、电子-8.22%，受大宗回落、美国抛储、新
能源链回调和成长估值回调影响。节后两日风格快速反向切换：10月 8
日资源+防御占优，10月 9 日转为 AI 应用+大金融领涨，存量资金高频
轮动。 
◼ 宏观背景：海内外约束的边际变化： 
◼ 海外 2026年 9月全球加息共振，美联储加息至 3.75%-4.00%，日央行、
澳洲联储同步收紧；9 月底 PCE 低于预期带来松动，但 10 月初纪要放
鹰、沃勒称仍需加息，美债 10Y 升至 5.365%创二十余年新高，海外约
束“鹰派再强化”。国内财政金融协同组合拳落地：房贷贴息、PSL降
息扩围六张网、再贷款加码、1.2 万亿买断式逆回购，政策精准克制，
提供政策底但总量β有限。10 月 9 日《新质生产力意见》最高级别定
调。 
◼ 市场核心分歧解析： 
◼ 核心分歧集中于七问：2026 年 9 月普跌主因是海外高利率导致估值回
调，而非国内基本面恶化；微盘逆势是存量资金抱团，风险度107.45处
高位，10月 9日首现“局部顶左侧信号”；地产是精准托底而非大水漫
灌，总量β有限、结构α主导；价值跑赢成长是短期避险，若加息预期
降温，成长有超跌修复弹性；10月倾向震荡分化；新质生产力《意见》
为方向性中长期加持、非短期单向利好；有色下跌由大宗回落和美国抛
储驱动，能源强、金属弱，反转未现。 
◼ 10月展望：五维度框架： 
◼ 五维框架中，F2 政策+海外定方向，F1 风险收益定下行空间，F3 资金
博弈定上行斜率，F4 日历效应供概率，F5 情景给结论。万得全 A 风险
度处低位，大盘成长3%-4%分位超跌，微盘97%分位拥挤。政策托底与
海外高利率拉锯，缩量存量博弈限制普涨。历史大幅回调后次月胜率
57%、2020年以来胜率 73%。2026年 10月起，基准是震荡承压；美债
回落且政策加码则反弹，加息且地缘升级则下行，霍尔木兹封锁或地产
信用风险为小概率尾部。 
◼ 核心结论与总结 
◼ 2026年 9月核心是海外利率上行估值回调+结构分化，国内 PMI尚稳，
是估值回调而非业绩走弱。10 月 8 日延续弱势但防御拓宽，10 月 9 日
探底回升、成交1.92万亿，AI 应用+大金融领涨，美债长端回落提供外
部条件。10月基准仍是震荡、 结构分化， 但短期不必过度悲观。 政策底、
风险度低位与美债长端回落已提供超跌反弹线索；若后续美债 10Y 不
再创新高、成交站稳 1.9万亿上方，市场存在超跌反弹窗口，对短期修
复可适度乐观一些。中期趋势反转仍需利率拐点与增量资金确认。风格
高频轮动、持续性弱。关键跟踪美债 10Y、FOMC、成交额、三季报、
地缘、AI延续性、微盘动量。 
◼ 风险提示：美联储超预期加息；地缘冲突升级；微盘拥挤度与局部顶风
险；政策效果不及预期；政策预期落空风险；数据口径风险。整体需关
注海外利率、地缘、微盘拥挤与政策兑现。

1.   2026 年 9月行情复盘：海外估值调整下的结构性分化 
1.1.   传导闭环：为什么"估值回调"会演变为"结构分化" 
 2026年 9 月 A股的回调与分化，拆开看是一条四步传导链： 
【利率端】美联储 2026年 9月 16日加息→美债 10Y收益率逼近 5%上方 → 【估
值端】"长久期资产"（大盘成长/白马）贴现率抬升→估值承压+外资流出 → 【资金端】
内资存量资金避开高波动的大盘品种 → 【抱团端】转向低流动性、低机构持仓的微盘
股抱团→大小盘剪刀差走阔 
 这条链的每一环都有数据支撑： 
【利率端】：2026 年 9 月美国 10 年期国债收益率一度逼近 5%，全球无风险利率
中枢上移，是 2026年 9月 A 股估值的最大压制源；进入10月，10年期收益率进一步
升至 5.365%（2002年 4 月以来最高），约束尚未缓解，边际上略有增强； 
【估值端】：沪深 300、创业板指风险度分别跌至自成立以来 3%、4%分位，大盘
成长类资产回调到了历史较低位置，说明贴现率抬升对长久期资产的冲击明显； 
【抱团端】：截至2026年 9 月 30日，万得微盘股指数风险度升至自成立以来97%
分位，与大盘形成结构性分化。 
 根源是利率敏感度的差异：大盘成长久期长、机构持仓重、外资定价比例高，对
贴现率最敏感，利率上行期首当其冲；微盘股短久期、内资定价、低机构持仓，几乎不
受外资流出和贴现率抬升的直接影响，反而成了存量资金在利率上行期的避风港。 
 2026年 9 月普跌源于海外利率上行估值回调， 而非国内基本面恶化； 分化的根源
是长久期与短久期资产的利率敏感度差异，在存量博弈中被资金行为放大。

1.2.   三段式走势：月初震荡、月中反抽、月末回调 
2026 年 9 月万得全 A 的回调并非一路向下，而是清晰的"三段式"结构（数据截至 
2026年 9月 30日）： 
表1：9 月万得全 A 三段式走势（截至 2026/09/30） 
阶段 区间 涨跌 特征 
月初震荡 9/1-9/10 -1.49% 缩量阴跌，方向不明 
月中反抽 9/11-9/20 +1.70% 政策预期升温，短暂修复 
月末回调 9/21-9/30 -4.58% 两根大阴线，对整体走势影响较大 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所

### Page 5

5 / 25 
月末急跌是 2026 年 9 月下跌的主要贡献段：2026 年 9 月 24 日 单日下跌 1.83%、
2026 年 9 月 28 日 单日下跌 2.74%。月末急跌的触发因素，一是海外加息预期在 2026
年 9月下旬反复 （2026年 10月加息概率一度回升） ， 二是国庆长假前的避险资金撤离，
三是微盘抱团在月末出现松动迹象。全月最高收盘 6507.99（2026年 9 月 1 日）、最低
收盘 6176.22（2026年 9月 28日），月内振幅 5.37%。 
  这一走势结构提示两个关键信息：其一，2026年 9 月下跌的"质"并不健康——
它不是温和的价值回归， 而是月末集中宣泄， 情绪宣泄后往往伴随超跌修复需求； 其二，
月末急跌恰逢政策组合拳密集落地（2026年 9月 29日 PSL降息等），政策对冲与情绪
宣泄在 2026年 9月 30日 前后形成了阶段性对冲。

1.3.   大小盘剪刀差分化 
2026 年 9 月市值从大到小，跌幅逐级收窄、直至微盘转正，是典型的"流动性收缩
下的资金抱团"。从沪深 300 的下跌 5.78%、到中证 500 的下跌 6.50%、再到国证 2000
的下跌 4.69%，最后到万得微盘股指数的上涨 1.11%，"越小越抗跌"的单调排列说明资
金持续向最小市值、最低流动性的角落收缩。不过要看到，微盘股风险度已升至 107.45
（2026年 10月 9日），处自成立以来高位（历史上仅约3%的时间更高），且于当日首
次触发"局部顶左侧信号"，它的抗跌反映的是拥挤度处于历史高位、而非安全边际——
抗跌的成色与持续性，要打个问号。

1.4.   10 月初至今（截至 2026年 10 月 9 日）的延续

### Page 6

6 / 25 
国庆节后首个交易日（2026年 10月 8日），A 股承接节前走势、回调开局：万得
全 A 下跌 1.614%、收于 6095.79，成交额放量至 16943 亿元（较 2026 年 9 月 30 日的
14501亿元放大约 17%） ， 显示节后资金有所回流， 但更多体现为存量资金兑现与调仓，
增量做多意愿尚不明显。 
结构上，10月 8日的分化较 9 月末更明显， 且风格天平向"资源+防御"进一步倾斜： 
抗跌端： 红利指数上涨1.42%、 万得微盘股指数仅跌0.33%， 石油石化 （上涨2.46%）、
公用事业（上涨 1.64%）、交通运输（上涨1.33%）、煤炭（上涨1.15%）、银行（上涨
1.03%）领涨——"高股息+资源+防御"成为节后首日的避风港； 
回调端：科创50下跌 4.82%、创业板指下跌3.15%，电子（下跌4.25%）、通信（下
跌 3.90%）、计算机（下跌 2.02%）领跌——成长股在美债收益率创新高的背景下继续
回调估值。 
10 月首个交易日延续了 9 月的核心矛盾（海外利率压制成长），但风格从"微盘独
强"演变为"红利+资源+微盘共同防御、成长补跌"，防御的广度扩大，反映市场对海外利
率风险的定价较 9月末更充分。 
次日（2026年 10月 9日）市场出现方向相反的修复：万得全A 早盘一度下行、随
后逐级回升，全天收涨0.184%、收于6107.00，成交额放量至19167亿元（较10月 8日
增加 2224 亿元、增幅 13.1%），全市场超 3200 只个股上涨、69只涨停、封板率 83%；
创业板指 3000 点大关失而复得。结构上，主线发生了两个交易日内少见的快速切换：
领涨端由前一日的资源/防御，切换为 AI 应用（传媒上涨 5.28%、计算机上涨 2.33%）
与大金融（非银金融上涨 1.61%），商贸零售和有色金属紧随其后；而前一日的领涨端
则普遍回落（银行下跌 1.07%、石油石化下跌 0.20%、公用事业上涨 0.06%），前一日
的领跌端多数止跌（电子下跌 1.47%、通信下跌 0.86%，跌幅收窄），但国防军工反而
领跌 1.70%。 
两个交易日的组合信息量大于单日：10月 8 日是"资源+防御占优、 成长估值回调"，
10月 9日则反转为"AI应用+大金融领涨、资源/防御退潮"。这种快速反向切换说明，在
当前"存量博弈+政策与海外消息面高频扰动"的环境下，单一交易日的风格信号稳定性
低，不宜据某一日结构外推中期趋势；但两日共同提供的确切信息是：资金并未系统性
撤离，而是在"高股息防御"与"政策/AI 主题"之间高频轮动——这正是"跷跷板轮动、非
普涨"格局的实证。

2.   行业分化：政策主线 vs 周期回调 
2026年 9月行业分化的核心是两条线：政策托底线（房地产、银行、医药）逆势上
涨，周期/成长线（有色、电力设备、机械设备）回调。行业的分化比大盘更明显，也更

### Page 7

7 / 25 
能揭示资金的真实去向。

2.1.   典型行业深度归因：房地产 vs 有色金属 
2026年 9月最值得深挖的两个行业，一个是政策驱动的房地产（上涨6.28%），一
个是周期回调的有色金属（下跌 12.87%），两者恰好是"政策主线 vs 周期回调"的典型
样本。 
房地产（上涨6.28%）：政策驱动的逆势上涨。 从基本面看，房地产的上涨并非销
售数据的真实回暖，而是政策预期的集中定价——月末"财政金融协同组合拳"落地，房
贷贴息（首套+120 ㎡+150万三门槛）直指刚需购房成本，PSL 降息（1.75%→1.5%）并
扩围至"六张网"新基建，银行息差保护预期也带动了银行股同步走强。从资金面看，房
地产是 2026年 9月政策边际变化最大的方向， 吸引了存量资金中"赌政策"的那一部分。
但要清醒认识到：政策是结构性的（精准托底而非大水漫灌），房地产的上涨更多是"预
期先行"，后续的持续性取决于政策实效能否落到基本面数据上——而截至 2026 年 10
月 9 日，节后两日房地产板块分别下跌 1.17%、0.24%，说明政策预期在假期后出现回
吐、且尚未企稳，预期先行后的验证压力已经显现。 
有色金属（下跌 12.87%）：周期回调的典型。 直接压力来自大宗商品回落与美国
抛储 4000 万桶（WTI 跌破 90 美元），油价下行通过"通胀预期回落→大宗商品估值

### Page 8

8 / 25 
下修"的链条压低了有色、油气等资源品的价格中枢。更深层的逻辑是，美联储加息周期
下全球需求预期走弱，叠加美元走强，共同压制了以美元计价的工业金属。有色企稳的
关键不在 A 股内部，而在全球需求与美元走向，短期看趋势性反转信号尚未出现。不
过 10月 8日石油石化逆势上涨 2.46%，反映地缘冲突（霍尔木兹海峡、俄乌）推升的原
油风险溢价开始被定价，能源板块与工业金属出现分化。

2.2.   节后两个交易日行业表现：风格切换的实证 
节后首日行业表现清晰勾勒出"资源+防御 vs 成长"的对立：领涨的全部是低估值、
高股息、 与利率负相关度低的防御性/资源性行业； 领跌的则是对美债收益率最敏感的成
长行业（电子、通信、计算机）。值得注意的是，9月领涨的房地产（下跌1.17%）、医
药生物（下跌2.49%）在10月 8日均转为下跌，说明9 月的"政策预期交易"在节后出现
退潮，市场重新回到"海外利率定价"的主逻辑上。 
但 2026年 10月 9 日这一格局被迅速改写：传媒单日上涨5.28%（AI 应用、影视院
线午后涨停潮，新华传媒 9 连板）、计算机上涨 2.33%、商贸零售上涨 2.30%、有色金
属上涨 2.17%、非银金融上涨 1.61% 领涨；国防军工下跌 1.70%、电子下跌 1.47%、银
行下跌 1.07% 领跌。前一日的防御端（银行、石油石化、公用事业）集体转为滞涨或下
跌，而前一日的领跌端（传媒、计算机）则大幅反弹——行业主线在两个交易日内完成
一次完整的反向切换。 
行业分化的主线从 9 月的"政策托底 vs 周期回调"，在 10 月首日演变为"资源+防
御 vs 成长估值回调"，又在 10 月 9 日反转为"AI 应用+大金融 vs 资源/防御退潮"。三
个阶段的共同底色是存量资金在有限方向间的高频轮动：政策预期交易在节后退潮较早
（地产、医药），地缘驱动能源链只在10月 8日独立走强一日，AI 应用则在 10月 9日
接力成为新的资金焦点。对判断的含义是：行业主线的持续性偏弱，单日排名变化不足
以确认中期趋势，需以成交额能否持续放大、以及 AI 应用能否从主题走向业绩兑现为
观察锚。

3.   宏观背景：海内外约束的边际变化 
3.1.   海外：从"边际松动"到"鹰派再强化" 
2026 年 9 月主基调是全球加息共振——美联储 2026 年 9 月 16 日 加息 25bp 至 
3.75%-4.00%、日央行 2026年 9月 18日 加息至 1.25%（31 年高位）、澳洲联储 2026
年 9月 29日 第四次加息至 4.60%，全球主要央行的无风险利率中枢集体上移，构成了
压制 A 股估值的最大外部约束。 
9 月底曾出现三个关键的边际松动信号：① 2026 年 8 月 PCE 低于市场一致预期
（2026 年 9 月 30 日 公布），核心 PCE 同比 3.0%（市场一致预期 3.3%），2026 年
10月加息概率一度从 50.9% 降至 37.1%；② 威廉姆斯（美联储三把手）偏鸽表态"年
内仅需加息一次、2026 年 10 月不必急"；③ 美国抛储 4000 万桶，WTI 在 2026 年 9
月跌破 90美元，缓解输入性通胀压力。 
但进入 10月，这一"边际松动"被逆转： 
美联储 9月会议纪要放鹰（2026年 10月 7日公布）：显示9月加息背后是"鹰派共
识"， 多数官员倾向年内再加息一次， 仅未明确时点； 理事沃勒表态"仍需更多加息"（2026
年 10月 8日）：明确"需进一步加息以更快压低通胀，但加息节奏保持灵活、无需连续
会议进行"——方向未变、时点弹性加大；美债收益率创二十余年新高：截至2026年 10
月 7 日收盘，10 年期国债收益率升至 5.365%（2002 年 4 月以来最高）、30 年期升至
5.732%（2002年 5 月以来最高），美国 30年期固定抵押贷款利率已达 7%上方。 
结论：海外约束从 9月底的"边际缓和"逆转为 10月初的"鹰派再强化"，A股拿到

### Page 10

10 / 25 
的不再是"喘息窗口"， 而是"高利率更久"的再度确认。 这直接解释了节后首日成长股的
补跌——美债收益率创新高，长久期资产的贴现率压力重新加大。 
2026年 10月 9日出现本轮的第一个反向信号：美债长端收益率自高位回落，30年
期回落至 5.6024%、10年期回落至 5.235%，"创新高"的定价出现短暂修正，这是当日A
股探底回升、成长股止跌（科创50由前一日下跌 4.82%转为上涨 0.06%）的直接外部条
件。但需审慎看待这一回落：其一，单日回落不构成趋势反转，美债10年期仍在 5.2%
上方的高位；其二，加息预期本身仍处拉锯状态 ——圣路易斯联储主席穆萨莱姆
（Musalem）称"未来 6 至 9 个月仍需加息"、富兰克林邓普顿CEO预计"12月加息"、摩
根士丹利则认为"十年来首次看多美债"、太平洋投资管理公司（PIMCO）提示"10 年期
有触及 6%的风险"，市场存在明显分歧。结论是：海外约束在 10 月 9 日出现边际缓和
的第一个信号，但尚未构成"利率拐点"的确认，需连续多个交易日的回落与加息预期下
修才能成立。

3.2.   国内：财政金融协同组合拳落地 + 节后资金面 
2026年 9月底至 10月初国内政策密集落地，构成 2026年 10月的政策底。与过去
几轮刺激相比，这轮政策的关键词是"精准、克制、有边界"，我们逐一拆解其突破与限
制：

节后资金面的新变化：2026年 10月 8 日，央行开展6060亿元隔夜逆回购操作、当
日净回笼 6085亿元，同时开展12000亿元买断式逆回购——"短期回笼+中期投放"的组
合，反映央行在维护流动性合理充裕的同时，避免短期资金过度淤积。2026 年 10 月 9
日，央行开展 20亿元 7天期逆回购操作（利率 1.40%）、当日净回笼 60亿元，短端资
金面维持平稳。此外，国庆假期楼市出现"看房热情回升"（开发商优惠活动带动），是
房贷贴息政策的初步效果，但成交数据的实质性验证仍需观察。 
这套政策传递的信号很清楚：没有动用"降 LPR、全面降准"这类总量工具，而是用
"财政贴息与结构性货币工具"的组合，方向是"稳地产、促新基建、保小微"。对市场而
言， 政策提供了"政策底"的确定性， 但因为是结构性的， 它提供的是结构 α 而非总量 β。 
需要正视的是：海外鹰派再强化（3.1节）大幅抬升了外部约束，政策底与海外压力的拉
锯中，政策底的"托而不举"特征更加明显。 
海外从"边际松动"逆转为"鹰派再强化"（美债创二十余年新高），国内政策底以"精
准克制"方式落地、节后以"短期回笼+中期投放"组合维护流动性；海内外约束的拉锯较
9 月底略有加大，市场情绪边际趋于谨慎，是 10 月展望需要下调乐观预期的核心原因。

3.3.   产业政策最高级别定调：《关于发展新质生产力的意见》 
2026 年 10 月 9 日 18时 10分，新华社受权发布中共中央、国务院印发的《关于发
展新质生产力的意见》。这是继 2025年 8 月国务院《"人工智能+"行动意见》之后，产
业政策层级的再一次跃升——由国务院层面上升为中共中央与国务院联合印发。 
文件共 5方面 19项举措：一是大力推进科技创新（5项）；二是推动科技创新和产
业创新深度融合（5项，含"全面实施人工智能+行动"）；三是着力推进发展方式创新（3
项）；四是扎实推进体制机制创新（4 项）；五是深化人才工作机制创新（2 项）。其
中，第（七）项提出培育壮大新兴产业，明确新一代信息技术、新能源、新材料、智能
网联新能源汽车、机器人、生物医药、高端装备、航空航天等方向，并强调避免"内卷式
"竞争、低空经济与商业航天健康有序发展；第（八）项前瞻布局未来产业，点名量子科
技、生物制造、氢能和核聚变能、脑机接口、具身智能、第六代移动通信（6G），并建

### Page 12

12 / 25 
立未来产业投入增长和风险分担机制；第（十）项要求全面实施"人工智能+"行动，覆盖
智能网联新能源汽车、AI手机和电脑、人形机器人等新一代智能终端；第（十六）项提
出加大金融创新产品供给，壮大耐心资本、支持优质科技型企业上市融资。 
与 2025 年 8 月《"人工智能+"行动意见》相比，本次文件的边际变化有三：其一，
层级升格（中共中央与国务院联合印发）；其二，范围扩展（从"人工智能+"单一主线扩
展至覆盖科技创新、产业创新、发展方式、体制机制、人才机制的全链条）；其三，同
时设定约束条件——明确"不能脱实向虚、贪大求全、搞泡沫化"，严禁借发展新质生产
力之名违规滥用政策，对"一哄而上、盲目投资"造成重大损失的严肃追责。 
对市场而言，这一文件的意义是方向性的中长期加持、但伴随反泡沫约束：一方面
确立了 AI 应用、未来产业（量子科技、具身智能、6G、核聚变）的中长期政策主线，
为成长方向提供了顶层背书；另一方面"反泡沫、防一哄而上"的表述对纯题材炒作构成
约束。需要特别指出，文件于2026年 10月 9日 18时 10分发布、属盘后事件——当日
传媒板块上涨 5.28%主要由 AI 应用产业催化（AI 短剧、影视院线）驱动，并非直接源
自本文件。这构成一个重要的可验证节点：政策级利好在下一交易日的兑现程度，将检
验当前"主题行情能否转化为持续主线"的成色。

4.   市场核心分歧解析 
4.1.   核心分歧与我们的回答 
下表提炼了 2026年 9 月收官、10月初开局后市场最具争议的七个问题，并给出我
们的判断（预期差所在）： 
表7：核心分歧与我们的回答（数据截至 2026 年 10 月 9日）

什么普跌？ 
海外"高利率更久"是主因，而非国内基本面恶化——美联储加息、
日央行加息至31年高位，全球无风险利率上行压制估值；国内
PMI（采购经理指数）尚稳，是"估值回调"而非"业绩疲软"

纯资金抱团，基本面无支撑——成交萎缩下量化/游资在低流动性

35.69，拥挤度与动能背离，需警惕高位小票补跌

1.17%，政策预期已现退潮，验证"总量β有限、结构α主导"

随利率路径走，10月初天平偏向价值/红利——9月底曾预期"利率

12.87%）的下
跌结束了吗？ 
能源链与工业金属分化——大宗回落+美国抛储压制工业金属，但
地缘（霍尔木兹、俄乌）推升原油，10月 8日石油石化上涨
2.46%；有色企稳需看全球需求与美元走向，短期趋势性反转信号
未现 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所

4.2.   关键分歧的深入解读 
分歧一：是"估值回调"还是"业绩疲软"？ 这是我们与市场最大的预期差。2026年 9
月 A 股的普跌，市场容易归因于"国内基本面走弱"，但证据并不支持：2026年 9 月官
方制造业 PMI 为 50.1%，环比回升 0.3 个百分点、重回荣枯线上方，制造业景气边际
改善；社融与信贷结构同样保持平稳。既然国内基本面并未走坏，下跌的根源就只能来
自估值端——全球无风险利率上行推高了大盘成长类资产的贴现率。"估值回调"意味着
一旦利率预期边际松动，估值修复的弹性会来得比 "业绩走弱"更快、更直接——不过，
10月初美联储纪要偏鹰、沃勒提及仍需加息、美债收益率创二十余年新高，显示“利率
边际松动”的假设暂未得到印证，这也是我们适度下调 10月乐观预期的主要原因。 
分歧二：微盘股的强势能否延续？ 万得微盘股指数 2026 年 9 月逆势上涨 1.11%、
10月 8日仅跌 0.33%、10月 9日再涨 0.35%，是抗跌性最强的宽基，但我们对这一强势
的可持续性保持警惕。从机制上看，微盘股的抗跌并非基本面驱动，而是"存量博弈下的
资金抱团"——10月 9日万得全 A成交放量至 19167亿元，但增量资金相对有限，量化
与游资在低流动性、低机构持仓的微盘股中抱团取暖。问题在于，这一抱团已把微盘股
风险度推升至 107.45、处于自成立以来高位，且于 2026 年 10 月 9 日首次触发"局部顶
左侧信号"（短期动量由 62.94骤降至 35.69），安全边际较薄。我们不做"微盘即将见顶
"的方向判断，但可以确定的是：风险度高位是微盘长期上涨偏拥挤的结构常态、并非择
时信号； 微盘真正的回撤触发在于①2026年 12月日历效应 （财务暴雷+机构排名考核）
②绝对主线出现虹吸资金——当前 10月 9 日 AI应用+大金融的快速走强， 已具备"潜在
虹吸源"的雏形，这是相较 10月 8 日新增的边际风险，需持续观察。 
分歧三： 房地产反弹是"政策底"还是"反弹卖点"？ 房地产 2026年 9月上涨 6.28%，
是申万一级行业中的正收益明显的行业，直接触发因素是月末 "财政金融协同组合拳"。

### Page 14

14 / 25 
但我们强调，这轮政策是"精准托底"而非"大水漫灌"：房贷贴息设定了首套、120 ㎡、
150 万三门槛，PSL 投放方向扩展至"六张网"，再贷款也是定向的，而非全面降 LPR。
这种结构化的政策设计，决定了它的总量 β 有限，更多体现在结构 α 上。10月 8日
房地产板块下跌 1.17%，医药生物下跌 2.49%，恰好印证了"政策预期先行、退潮同样迅
速"的判断——3.1节的海外鹰派再强化削弱了政策底的相对吸引力，政策链的行情需要
基本面数据来重新支撑。 
分歧四：价值/红利跑赢成长能持续吗？ 2026年 9月大盘价值（银行、红利）逆势
抗跌，10月 8 日红利指数进一步上涨 1.42%，是"高利率估值回调"的镜像——大盘成长
久期长、对贴现率最敏感，被资金抛弃得最狠，避险资金被动流入价值。这一风格的持
续性取决于利率路径：9月底我们曾判断"若海外加息预期降温， 成长有超跌修复弹性"，
但 10 月初美债收益率创二十余年新高，这一前提被打破。修正后的判断是：在美债收
益率确认回落之前，价值/红利的防御属性仍占优，成长（创业板指/科创 50风险度处自
成立以来 3-4%分位）的修复弹性需等待利率拐点确认，而非简单依据"超跌"。 
分歧五：2026年 10月延续下跌还是修复？ 市场在这个问题上分歧最大： 看空者强
调宏观择时模型 -1 分、海外鹰派再强化（美债5.365%）、地缘风险；看多者强调政策
底、大跌后次月历史胜率、风险度低位。2026年 10月 9 日的深 V 反转（万得全A 上涨
0.184%、成交放量至 19167亿元、美债长端回落）为"修复"一侧提供了当日证据，但单
日反转不足以推翻中期约束。综合判断是震荡、结构分化，但"上有顶"的天花板较 9月
底预期下移——下有政策底与风险度低位（万得全 A 风险度 16.66）封住下行，上有海
外高利率与存量博弈限制上行。既不会重演 9 月的单边下跌，也难以出现趋势性上涨，
基准是"下有底、上有顶（且顶下移）"的震荡格局（详见第五部分五维框架）。 
分歧六：有色/能源的下跌结束了吗？ 有色金属是 2026 年 9 月领跌板块（下跌
12.87%），直接压力是大宗商品回落与美国抛储4000万桶（WTI跌破 90美元）。但10
月出现关键分化：工业金属（铜、铝）仍受美元走强与全球需求走弱压制，而能源（石
油石化）受地缘冲突（霍尔木兹海峡油轮遇袭、俄乌升级）推升，10月 8日石油石化逆
势上涨 2.46%。我们的判断是：能源链的风险溢价已开始独立定价，工业金属仍需等待
全球需求与美元拐点，有色板块内部的"能源强、金属弱"分化将延续。

5.   2026 年 10月展望 
5.1.   量化模型：权益综合信号（宽基方向） 
"权益综合信号"月频量化择时模型以基本面、资金面、国际面、估值面、技术面五
维度打分合成综合信号（正看多、负看空、0中性），据以判断万得全A 月度方向，样
本自 2016年 1月起、数据截至 2026年 9月 30日。

信号解读与张力：2026年 10 月综合信号 -1（偏弱）。历史回测显示 -1 的"看跌"
成色并不强——14次中出现正收益 8次（概率 57.14%）、平均收益率仅 -1.14%，说明 
-1 更多是"偏弱"。结合海外鹰派再强化（美债10年期 5.365%创二十余年新高、10 月 9
日小幅回落至 5.235%）、政策底、风险度低位（万得全 A 风险度 16.66），综合判断：
模型信号"偏弱"、结构数据"下有支撑"、海外约束"边际加重但 10 月 9 日首现缓和信号
"，三者叠加指向"震荡承压、结构分化"——10月或非单边下跌（政策底+风险度低位支
撑，10月 9日深 V已验证），但上行受海外高利率强约束，基准是"上有顶、下有底"的
震荡，且顶较 9月底预期下移。

5.2.   宽基方向：五维框架 
五个维度并非平行，其中 F2（政策+海外）主导方向，F1 看下行空间，F3 看上行
斜率，F4、F5 分别提供概率参考与最终的情景加权。落到 2026 年 10 月（自 2026 年
10月 9日起算）：F2 的政策托底与海外鹰派再强化拉锯，且海外一侧加重，决定了"非
单边、且偏承压"；F1 风险度低位封住下行空间；F3 节后放量但增量不足限制上行斜

### Page 16

16 / 25 
率；F4 大跌后次月偏反弹提供概率支撑（弱化）；F5 综合为"震荡、 结构分化"的基准。 
F1 风险收益结构：整体低位、结构拥挤（含历史分位） 
图1：万得全 A 指数风险趋势模型（2026/10/09）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所

万得全 A 风险度 16.66（处自成立以来约 20%分位），上证指数 27.55 ——整体处
在历史偏低位置，下行空间有限。从历史对标看，当前万得全 A 风险度 16.66 与历史
上几个典型的"阶段底部区域"处于同一水平：2022年 11月上旬（风险度约 18.8，对应
2022年 10月恐慌底后的修复起点）、2020年 4月（风险度约 23.1，对应疫情底 V 型
反转起点）、2016年 3 月（风险度约 21.0，对应熔断后修复）。这些时点的共同特征是
"风险度处于 20附近或以下的中低区、之后往往迎来修复行情"，但只是概率参考，并非
必然。 
但结构上分化明显： 
超跌端：沪深 300风险度 -3.43（超跌状态）、创业板指 -2.96（超跌状态）、科创
50 -4.78（超跌状态）——大盘成长类已处历史低位，安全边际厚，这是10月"超跌修复
"弹性的来源（前提是海外利率不再上行）；10月 9日超跌端已出现修复迹象（科创 50
由前一日下跌 4.82%转为上涨 0.06%、创业板指上涨 0.22%）； 
拥挤端：微盘股 107.45（超涨状态，自成立以来高位）、红利指数 81.23（高风险）
——拥挤度高、安全边际薄；微盘股于 10月 9 日首次触发"局部顶左侧信号"。

### Page 17

17 / 25 
结论：大盘整体安全边际厚，但"超跌的结构"（沪深 300/创业板/科创 50）与"高位
常态的结构"（微盘/红利）并存——超跌成长有修复需求，但需海外利率配合；微盘/红
利风险度高属上涨常态、 非择时信号，但微盘的"局部顶左侧信号"与红利拥挤度均需纳
入观察，真正风险在 2026年 12月日历效应与主线虹吸。 
F2 主导逻辑边际变化：政策托底 vs 海外鹰派再强化（海外加重） 
强化项（国内）：财政金融协同组合拳落地、2026年 10 月 8日央行 12000亿买断
式逆回购、2026年 10月 9 日中共中央与国务院印发《关于发展新质生产力的意见》—
—国内政策面边际友好， 是10月的托底力量， 且产业政策为成长方向提供了顶层背书； 
加重项（海外）：美联储9月纪要鹰派、沃勒"仍需加息"、美债 10年期收益率升至
5.365%（2002 年 4 月以来最高）——海外约束从 9 月底的"边际松动"逆转为"鹰派再强
化"，是本轮展望最重要的边际变化； 
缓和信号（海外，新增）：2026年 10月 9 日美债长端收益率自高位回落（30年期
约-7 个基点、10 年期回落至 5.235%），是"鹰派再强化"之后的第一个反向信号，但尚
不构成利率拐点； 
加剧项（地缘）：俄乌冲突升级（克拉马托尔斯克 30 死）、霍尔木兹海峡油轮遇
袭；2026年 10 月 8 日特朗普表示中期选举前不攻击伊朗、油价回落——地缘风险溢价
短期缓和、但选举后不确定性仍高。 
结论：主导逻辑从 9 月底的"政策托底 vs 高利率（边际缓和）"，演变为 10 月的
"政策托底+产业政策加持 vs 高利率（再强化）+地缘风险"，并在10月 9日出现"产业
政策利好+美债回落"的双重边际改善。 核心含义是：10月不会像 9月那样单边下跌 （有
政策底与风险度低位），但上行天花板较9月底预期明显下移（海外鹰派再强化），方
向是"下有底、上有顶（顶下移）"的震荡；10月 9日结构上 AI应用/大金融占优、资源
/防御退潮，风格切换的确认尚需观察。 
F3 资金博弈结构：节后放量、增量仍不足 
2026 年 9月是缩量调整——万得全 A成交额从 2026年 8 月日均 22546 亿元降至 
2026年 9月日均 18315 亿元， 环比下降18.8%；2026年 9 月 30日 当日成交 14501 亿
元，处近 1 年 0.8% 分位的低位。10 月 8 日节后首日成交额回升至 16943 亿元（较 9
月 30日放大约 17%） ， 但需注意： 这一放量伴随的是指数下跌1.614%，属"放量下跌"，
更可能是前期获利盘/避险盘兑现，而非增量资金入场。10 月 9 日成交额进一步放大至
19167亿元（较 10月 8日增加 2224亿元、增幅 13.1%），且伴随指数上涨 0.184%、超
3200只个股上涨、ETF持续放量——这是节后首次出现"放量上涨"，指向资金承接意愿
的边际改善，但单日尚不足以确认增量资金系统性入场。10月的资金变量多空交织： 
潜在增量：2026年 10月 8日央行净回笼 6085亿元（短期收紧）但12000亿买断式

### Page 18

18 / 25 
逆回购投放（中期对冲）；房贷贴息落地（政策驱动的结构性增量）； 
潜在减量：微盘/红利拥挤度高位（若 12月日历效应或主线虹吸引发抱团松动，则
有资金流出）、海外若超预期加息引发外资流出、地缘风险下的避险情绪。 
结论：资金面大概率维持"存量博弈为主、政策驱动的结构性增量"格局。普涨缺乏
资金基础，结构行情是主基调——10 月即便修复，也更可能是结构性的（资源/防御占
优），而非全面普涨。 
F4 日历效应与均值回归 
基于万得全 A 指数 （881001.WI） 日频原始数据 （2000-01 至 2026-09，共321个月）
实测： 
表11：大幅回调月后次月日历效应统计（样本区间 2000-01 至 2026-09） 
统计 样本 结果 
单月大跌≤-5%后次月 65 次（全样本） 胜率57%、次月均值+0.5%；2020以来胜率73%、均值+2.4% 
单月大涨≥5%后次月 75 次（全样本） 本月中位+9.7% → 次月中位+2.3%（涨幅显著收敛） 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
结论：2026年 9月万得全 A指数回调 5.31%。历史规律显示"回调幅度较大后次月
偏反弹"——全样本胜率 57%、次月均值上涨 0.5%，近 6 年（2020 以来）胜率升至
73%、均值上涨 2.4%。但需警惕：日历效应在"海外利率系统性上行"的环境下会明显
弱化（如 2022年多次大跌后次月仍疲弱），10月初美债创二十余年新高，故本次日历
效应的参考权重应下调。综合看，基准虽为震荡分化，但短期存在超跌反弹窗口：若美
债 10Y 回落至 5.0%下方并站稳、成交持续站上 1.9 万亿，修复弹性有望打开，对短期
行情可适度乐观一些。

5.3.   风格观察

表13：2026 年 10 月风格观察（自 2026-10-09） 
风格 方向判断 逻辑

风格结论：10月初"高利率更久"再度强化，风格天平明确偏向红利/价值（防御）+
微盘（抱团）+资源（地缘），成长（创业板/科创）承压。这与9 月底"利率降温→成长
修复"的乐观预期形成反差——修正的核心依据是美债收益率创新高。三条品种关系仍
成立：红利↔微盘同向、科技↔红利跷跷板、微盘↔科技存量博弈下跷跷板，合起来是
跷跷板轮动、非普涨。 
微盘择时：微盘风险度高（107.45，自成立以来高位）是长期上涨偏拥挤的结构常
态、非择时信号；真正风险是①2026年 12 月日历效应（财务暴雷+机构排名）②绝对
主线虹吸。相较 10月 8日，10月 9 日出现两个新增的边际变化：其一，微盘首次触发
"局部顶左侧信号"（短期动量 62.94→35.69）；其二，AI 应用+大金融快速走强，已具备
"潜在虹吸源"雏形。 两者均不构成"微盘见顶"的充分条件， 但拥挤度高位下需提高警惕、
并密切跟踪信号验证。

### Page 20

20 / 25 
风格切换量化确认信号：①微盘股风险度回落至 90以下；②创业板指综合动量回
升至 40以上；③单日成交额持续在 1.9万亿上方——满足 2个及以上可确认风格切换。

5.4.   行业观察（产业链基本面五条线索） 
线索一： 资源/能源链 （石油石化+煤炭+有色）——地缘冲突 （霍尔木兹海峡、 俄乌）
推升原油风险溢价，石油石化 10 月 8 日上涨 2.46%，但 10 月 9 日转为下跌 0.20%；同
时有色金属 10月 9日上涨 2.17%、煤炭上涨1.57%，反映资源链内部向工业金属与煤炭
扩散。资源链是 10月 8日最确定的边际改善方向，但 10月 9日部分回吐。 
线索二：防御/红利链（银行+公用事业+交通运输）——高利率环境下类债属性占
优，10 月 8 日银行上涨 1.03%、公用事业上涨 1.64%、交通运输上涨 1.33%，验证防御
逻辑。 
线索三：政策托底链（地产+新基建）——房贷贴息、PSL六张网已落地，但 10月
8日房地产下跌 1.17%，政策预期退潮，需基本面数据重新验证；新基建方向的算力网/
电网相对抗跌。 
线索四：超跌成长链（电子+电力设备+计算机）——风险度处历史低位（科创 50 -
4.78、创业板 -2.96超跌），10月 8 日电子下跌 4.25%、通信下跌 3.90%，但 10月 9日
出现分化： 软件与应用端 （计算机上涨2.33%）随AI应用走强， 硬件端 （电子下跌1.47%）
仍弱。超跌不等于反弹，修复需等美债利率拐点；10 月 9 日计算机的走强更多来自 AI
应用主题而非超跌修复。 
线索五：AI应用与未来产业链（传媒+计算机+通信设备）——2026年 10月 9日传
媒上涨 5.28%领涨全行业（AI短剧、影视院线涨停潮）、计算机上涨2.33%，是当日资
金主攻方向；叠加中共中央与国务院《关于发展新质生产力的意见》于当日18时 10分
盘后发布，明确"全面实施人工智能+行动"与量子科技、具身智能、6G、核聚变等未来
产业方向，政策与资金形成共振，构成 10 月最具弹性的主题线索。但需注意：当日行
情由产业催化驱动、非文件直接驱动，且文件同时含"反泡沫、防一哄而上"约束，主题
行情的持续性需以后续交易日的兑现程度和三季报业绩验证。 
三季报验证链（2026年 10月中下旬密集披露）：按"业绩确定性"分三类——①政
策驱动需兑现（地产链、AI算力），三季报是检验"政策预期能否落地业绩"的关卡；②
超跌但业绩稳健 （医药、 部分高端制造） ；③业绩下修风险 （周期品、 部分新能源细分） 。
截至 2026年 10月 9日，三季报预告尚未集中披露，以上为前瞻推演、非结论。

6.   核心结论与总结 
6.1.   复盘结论 
2026年 9月 A 股的核心特征是海外利率上行估值回调、叠加结构分化：全球加息
共振推高无风险利率，压制大盘成长估值，存量资金在缩量环境下抱团微盘，走出了"万
得全 A下跌 5.31%、万得微盘股指数却上涨1.11%"的剪刀差。国内基本面并未恶化（制
造业 PMI 重回荣枯线上方），是"估值回调"而非"业绩疲软"。10 月首个交易日（10月
8 日），这一格局延续但风格拓宽：万得全 A 再跌 1.614%，防御从"微盘独强"演变为"
红利+资源+微盘共同抗跌"，成长补跌（科创 50单日-4.82%），核心诱因是海外鹰派再
强化（美债创二十余年新高）。 
10 月 9 日出现本轮首个反向信号： 万得全A探底回升、 上涨0.184%收于 6107.00，
成交额放量至 19167亿元（较前一日增加 2224亿元），全市场超3200只个股上涨；领
涨主线由"资源+防御"切换为"AI 应用+大金融"（传媒上涨 5.28%、计算机上涨 2.33%、
非银金融上涨 1.61%），外部条件是美债长端收益率回落（30年期约-7个基点、10年期
回落至 5.235%）。但需注意，当日 18 时 10 分新华社发布《关于发展新质生产力的意
见》属盘后事件，传媒行情主要由AI应用产业催化驱动、非文件直接驱动；且电子（下
跌 1.47%）、国防军工（下跌1.70%）仍在调整，成长股内部的"应用强、硬件弱"分化明
显。单日深V反弹不构成趋势反转，但确认了在风险度低位与政策底支撑下，指数下行
具备承接力量。

### Page 22

22 / 25 
6.2.   展望结论 
2026年 10月是"政策托底+产业政策加持"与"海外高利率再强化"的拉锯，基准判断
为震荡、结构分化：下有政策底、产业政策（《关于发展新质生产力的意见》）与风险
度低位（万得全 A 风险度 16.66）封住下行空间，上有海外高利率（美债 10 年期仍在
5.2%上方）+存量博弈限制上行斜率。与 9 月底的乐观预期相比，10月展望的核心修正
是"上行天花板下移"——美债收益率创二十余年新高使"利率降温→成长修复"的假设一
度失效，10月 9日的长端回落是该假设修复的第一个信号、但尚未确认。结构上，10月
9 日"AI 应用+大金融"边际走强、红利/资源防御属性边际下降、微盘出现局部顶左侧信
号，风格较 10月 8 日发生明显切换；这是震荡而非单边反转，方向上"下有底、上有顶
（顶下移）"，且结构主线高频轮动、持续性偏弱。短期看，10月 9日深 V 反转、美债
长端回落与超跌成长止跌， 已给出超跌反弹的初步信号； 在政策底与风险度低位支撑下，
对短期修复可适度乐观一些，但不宜外推为趋势反转。

6.4.   关键跟踪变量 
2026年 10月判断的证伪与验证，取决于以下几个可跟踪的信号：① 美债 10年期

### Page 23

23 / 25 
收益率（站上 5.5%则压制加剧，跌破 5.0%并站稳则"利率拐点"确认、成长修复弹性打
开；10月 9日已回落至 5.235%）；② 2026年 10月 FOMC是否加息（若落地则基准情
景下修至"继续下行"）；③ 成交额能否持续站上 1.9万亿上方（观察增量资金信号，10
月 9日 19167亿需确认持续性）；④ 三季报景气兑现情况（验证政策驱动板块与AI 应
用的业绩支撑）；⑤ 地缘风险（霍尔木兹海峡通航状态、俄乌局势）；⑥ AI应用主线
的延续性（《关于发展新质生产力的意见》于 10 月 9 日盘后发布，兑现程度是政策定
价与主题持续性的关键检验）；⑦ 微盘股短期动量（10 月 9 日已由 62.94 降至 35.69，
若持续下行则确认"局部顶"）。

7.   风险提示 
1) 美联储超预期加息风险（概率中、影响高） 
若美联储在 2026 年 10 月或 12 月超预期加息，美债收益率与美元进一步上行，将
加重全球风险资产与 A 股估值的压制。跟踪预警指标为 CME 加息概率与美债 10 年期
收益率（若站上 5.5%则压制加剧）。 
2) 地缘冲突升级风险（概率中、影响高） 
若霍尔木兹海峡实质性受阻或俄乌冲突进一步扩大，原油与能源价格上行将强化全
球再通胀、推高利率中枢。跟踪预警指标为霍尔木兹通航状态与布伦特原油价格。 
3) 微盘拥挤度与局部顶风险（概率中、影响中） 
微盘股风险度升至 107.45（自成立以来高位），2026年 10月 9 日首次触发"局部顶
左侧信号"（短期动量由 62.94降至 35.69），叠加AI应用主线走强形成潜在虹吸；此外
2026 年 12 月面临财务暴雷与机构排名压力。跟踪预警指标为微盘短期动量能否企稳、
以及 AI 应用成交占比是否持续抬升。 
4) 政策效果不及预期风险（概率中、影响中） 
若房贷贴息等政策落地后地产成交未回暖（10月 8日地产已跌 1.17%），政策托底
逻辑将受挫。跟踪预警指标为地产成交与地产链景气的实际数据。 
5) 政策预期落空风险（概率中、影响中） 
10 月 9 日《关于发展新质生产力的意见》为盘后发布，若主题行情未能延续，或政
策落地节奏慢于预期（文件同时强调"不能脱实向虚、贪大求全、搞泡沫化"、严禁一哄
而上），则 AI应用等主题方向可能快速降温。 
6) 数据口径风险（概率低、影响低） 
行业/风格/指数涨跌为原始数据 2026 年 8 月 31日至 2026 年 10 月 9 日实算；风险

### Page 24

24 / 25 
度为因子数据 TR（各指数自成立以来分位）；美债收益率为 2026 年 10 月 9 日收盘口
径（10年期 5.235%，较 10月 7日高点 5.365%回落）。

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6 至 12 个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相
对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议。投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身
特定状况，如具体投资目的、财务状况以及特定需求等，并完整理解和使用本报告内容，不
应视本报告为做出投资决策的唯一因素。

⻩仁勋讲话后，早盘曾跌 9.5%CoreWeave 股价转涨。该司五年期 CDS 周四盘中升破 1190
基点，反映债务违约保护成本的指标创历史新⾼，部分公司债收益率升⾄ 15% ⾄ 16% 以
上。甲⻣⽂、亚⻢逊、⾕歌⺟公司、 Meta 的五年期 CDS 利差均处历史⾼位。德银分析师认
为，随着 AI 资本开⽀周期越来越多地由债务、⽽⾮⾃由现⾦流⽀撑，投资者必须更重视信
⽤市场的价格信号，整体⽽⾔， AI 相关融资成本尚不算⾼，但已有些令⼈担忧的迹象。
摘要：
CoreWeave 违约⻛险飙升⾄历史新⾼、信⽤市场隐含的未来五年违约概率超过六成之际，英伟
达 CEO ⻩仁勋出⾯ “ 灭⽕ ” ，称 CoreWeave 财务实⼒持续改善、融资成本正在下降，英伟达没有
必要为其兜底。
美东时间 10 ⽉ 15 ⽇周四，反映公司债务违约保护成本的指标 CoreWeave 五年期信⽤违约互换
（ CDS ）利差⼀度上升 74.5 个基点，达到 1194.5 个基点，创盘中历史新⾼。与此同时，该公司

部分债券收益率升⾄ 15% ⾄ 16% 以上，反映投资者对其偿债能⼒的担忧急剧升温。
⻩仁勋同⽇在瑞典出席活动期间接受媒体采访时表⽰： “CoreWeave 的实⼒与⽇俱增。 ” 他称，⾃
⼰对该公司持乐观态度，并指出，该司的 “ 资本成本实际上正在下降 ” 。对于市场关注的英伟达是
否需要为 CoreWeave 提供财务兜底，⻩仁勋明确表⽰，他并不认为有此必要。
周四， CoreWeave （ CRWV ）股价⼤幅低开 6.6% ，早盘跌幅⼀度扩⼤⾄ 9.5% ，⻩仁勋讲话后，
加速收窄跌幅，午盘转涨后持续上⾏，尾盘⽇内涨幅曾略超 6% ，较早盘刷新的⽇低反弹约
17% 。到收盘，股价涨逾 5.9% ，扭转三⽇连跌，本周内的累计跌幅收窄到 15% 左右。
更值得警惕的是，信⽤市场的紧张情绪在蔓延，显⽰投资者正重新评估 AI 基础设施投资背后的债
务⻛险。媒体指出，甲⻣⽂五年期 CDS 利差周四已接近 300 个基点，本周四⽇升约 31 个基点，和
亚⻢逊、⾕歌⺟公司 Alphabet 、 Meta 的相关利差均处于历史⾼位；软银五年期 CDS 利差⼀⽇扩

⼤ 24 个基点；英伟达、意法半导体和 SpaceX 的 CDS 利差也明显⾛⾼。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

五年期 CDS 突破 1190 基点， CoreWeave 债券遭抛售
CoreWeave 正遭遇股债双杀。
ICE Data Services 数据显⽰，周四 CoreWeave 五年期 CDS 利差盘中升⾄ 1194.5 个基点，创有记
录以来新⾼，相关定价对应的未来五年累计违约概率超过 60% 。 LSEG 及 S&P Global Market
Intelligence 数据显⽰， CoreWeave 的 CDS 利差周四盘中突破 1100 个基点，对应的五年期违约⻛
险约为 64% ，⾼于⼀年前约 30% 的⽔平。
CDS 是⽤于对冲债务违约⻛险的信⽤衍⽣品，其利差通常随市场对发⾏⼈偿债能⼒的担忧加剧
⽽上升。不过， CDS 隐含的违约概率取决于定价模型及相关假设，并不意味着公司未来五年实
际违约概率已被精确测定。
债券市场的表现同样严峻。彭博援引⾏情数据称，周四盘中， CoreWeave 于 2031 年到期、票⾯
利率为 9% 的债券价格下跌约 3.3 美分，跌⾄每美元⾯值 79.9 美分，收益率升⾄ 15.6% ；另⼀只票

⾯利率为 9.75% 、同样于 2031 年到期的债券下跌约 2.6 美分，跌⾄ 78.65 美分，收益率升⾄
16.2% 。
路透报道称， CoreWeave 于 2032 年到期的债券在 Tradeweb 平台上的交易价格约为每美元⾯值
78 美分，收益率超过 16% 。不同债券的到期⽇、票⾯利率和交易价格有所不同，但都显⽰出投
资者正在要求更⾼的⻛险补偿。
债券价格⼤幅低于⾯值、收益率飙升，意味着 CoreWeave 未来通过债券市场融资或再融资可能
⾯临更⾼成本。
CoreWeave 联合创始⼈ Brannin McBee ⽇前接受《⾦融时报》采访时表⽰，公司希望获得投资
级信⽤评级，从⽽打开更⼴泛的融资渠道，甚⾄能够借⼊ “ ⽆限 ” 规模的资⾦池。这⼀⽬标与当下
⾼企的债券收益率形成鲜明反差：在投资者对偿债能⼒的担忧尚未消退之际，公司能否以更低
成本持续获得融资，仍有待市场检验。
甲⻣⽂、软银等 AI 相关企业 CDS ⾛⾼，信⽤⻛险向⾏业扩散
CoreWeave 并⾮唯⼀遭遇信⽤市场压⼒的 AI 相关企业。

### Page 3

周四盘中，甲⻣⽂五年期 CDS 利差报 288 个基点，与前⼀交易⽇持平，但较⼀周前的 260 个基点
扩⼤ 28 个基点；软银五年期 CDS 利差单⽇扩⼤ 24 个基点⾄ 424 个基点。与此同时，英伟达、意
法半导体和 SpaceX 的 CDS 利差也明显⾛⾼。
据 LSEG 及 S&P Global Market Intelligence 数据，亚⻢逊、 Alphabet 和 Meta 的 CDS 利差也升⾄
历史⾼位，不过整体上升速度慢于甲⻣⽂和 CoreWeave 。这些科技巨头的信⽤保险成本同样受
到 AI 投资融资⻛险重估的影响。
CDS 利差衡量投资者为防范债务违约⻛险所需⽀付的成本。利差⾛阔意味着市场要求更⾼的⻛
险补偿，但不等于企业即将违约，也不能将不同公司的 CDS 利差直接视为可相互⽐较的违约概
率。
多家 AI 相关企业的信⽤保险成本上升，反映出投资者正在重新审视 AI 基础设施投资的融资⽅式。
甲⻣⽂等企业的⼤规模借款需求受到关注， CoreWeave 则因负债沉重、仍未实现盈利且⾼度依

赖外部融资，成为市场压⼒最集中的案例。
路透社指出，美国通胀数据⾼于预期、市场对未来利率⾛向的担忧升温，加剧了投资者对 AI 巨额
资本开⽀回报周期的疑虑。随着⾏业越来越多地依赖债务⽽⾮⾃由现⾦流为 AI 建设融资，市场关
注点正从算⼒需求增⻓本⾝，转向相关企业能否及时创造⾜够利润和现⾦流，以覆盖不断增加
的利息⽀出。
约 500 亿美元债务压顶， CoreWeave 的扩张模式遭拷问
CoreWeave ⾯临的核⼼问题，是资本开⽀增⻓与现⾦流、融资能⼒之间的平衡。
媒体指出，这家 AI 云计算公司背负近 500 亿美元债务，且仍处于亏损状态。公司依靠⼤规模采购
英伟达 GPU 、建设数据中⼼并扩充算⼒容量来满⾜客⼾需求，⽽这类投资通常需要在收⼊逐步
兑现之前投⼊⼤量资⾦。
CoreWeave 已⼤举增加资本开⽀，以抢占快速增⻓的 AI 算⼒市场。在需求强劲的阶段，举债扩
张能够帮助企业提前建设基础设施，并利⽤⻓期客⼾合同⽀撑投资计划。但数据中⼼建设周期
⻓、设备投⼊⼤，⼀旦融资成本上升、项⽬延期或客⼾需求增⻓不及预期，企业就可能⾯临资

⾦缺⼝和偿债压⼒。
CoreWeave 的问题因此不只是债务规模⾼，⽽是市场正在质疑其未来现⾦流能否覆盖资本开
⽀、利息⽀出及其他经营成本。债券收益率上升⼜会进⼀步抬⾼新增融资的⻔槛，使这⼀问题
更加突出。
CoreWeave 的管理层则强调订单储备充⾜，并认为市场对其财务状况存在误判。但订单储备并
不等同于已经收到的现⾦：⻓期合同能够提供⼀定的收⼊可⻅性，却不能⾃动消除建设成本超
⽀、交付延迟、融资条件变化及客⼾集中度等⻛险。投资者需要判断的是，这些合同最终能否
转化为⾜够的现⾦流和可持续利润。
英伟达为何出⾯ “ 灭⽕ ” ，供应商融资与客⼾需求交织
⻩仁勋公开为 CoreWeave 释放信⼼，背后是英伟达与这家 AI 云服务商之间紧密的商业和资本联
系。
英伟达持有 CoreWeave 约 10% 的股份，是其第⼆⼤股东。同时， CoreWeave 也是英伟达 GPU 的
重要采购⽅，其商业模式依赖采购英伟达芯⽚、建设数据中⼼，再将算⼒租赁给 AI 开发商和⼤型
科技企业。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
如果 CoreWeave 融资受阻，后续数据中⼼建设、 GPU 采购及算⼒交付都可能受到影响。即便现
有订单不会⽴即取消，融资成本上升也可能改变项⽬建设节奏和资本回报预期，进⽽影响市场
对英伟达 AI 需求增⻓的判断。
更敏感的问题在于，供应商投资客⼾、客⼾采购供应商产品的安排，容易引发市场对 “ 循环融资 ”
的担忧。
这类交易本⾝并不意味着存在不当⾏为，但当企业依赖借款购买供应商的产品，⽽供应商⼜持
有企业股权时，投资者会追问：产业链的增⻓究竟有多少来⾃最终客⼾的真实需求，⼜有多少
依赖融资推动的持续扩张？
⻩仁勋强调 CoreWeave 的融资成本正在下降，并明确表⽰⽆需英伟达兜底，既是在维护重要合
作伙伴的市场信⼼，也是在避免市场将双⽅的商业联系直接解读为英伟达必须承担 CoreWeave

债务⻛险。
不过，这番表态并不能替代对 CoreWeave 资产负债表的审视。英伟达持有股权并不等于对其债
务提供担保，⻩仁勋的乐观判断也⽆法直接解决公司的融资需求、利息负担和现⾦流问题。
AI 投资回报能否覆盖巨额资本开⽀，成为市场核⼼问题
CoreWeave 的信⽤⻛波正在成为 AI 基础设施投资的⼀次压⼒测试。
在⽣成式 AI 需求快速增⻓的背景下，科技企业和算⼒运营商⼤举建设数据中⼼，试图抢占未来市
场。但基础设施投⼊往往先于收⼊兑现，尤其是依赖外部融资的新型云服务商，需要不断借款
购买 GPU 、建设机房并⽀付能源和运营成本。
当利率预期上升、债券投资者要求更⾼⻛险补偿时，这套模式就会⾯临更严格的检验。融资成
本增加不仅会压缩利润，还可能迫使企业放慢扩建速度；如果扩建放缓，⼜可能影响未来收⼊
增⻓和既有投资的回报。
德意志银⾏策略师  Jim Reid 指出，这并⾮⾸次因 AI 的担忧⽽经历显著的市场动荡，但随着 AI 资
本开⽀周期越来越多地由债务、⽽⾮⾃由现⾦流⽀撑，投资者必须更加重视信⽤市场的价格信

号。 “ 整体⽽⾔， AI 相关的融资成本尚不算⾼，但在⼀些边缘领域已出现令⼈担忧的迹象。 ”
这⼀判断凸显了⾏业⾯临的关键⽭盾：算⼒需求增⻓，并不⾃动等于算⼒运营商能够获得⾜够
利润；订单增⻓，也不必然意味着债务能够得到轻松偿还。
对 CoreWeave ⽽⾔，真正的考验是能否控制债务增⻓、持续获得融资，并将庞⼤的订单储备转
化为可持续的经营现⾦流。对英伟达及整个 AI 产业链⽽⾔，市场关注点则正在从 “ 还需要多少算
⼒ ” ，转向 “ 谁来为算⼒建设买单，以及这些投资最终能否产⽣⾜够回报 ” 。
⻩仁勋试图安抚市场，但 CoreWeave 债券收益率⾼企、信⽤保险费率创纪录，以及甲⻣⽂、软
银等企业 CDS 利差⾛⾼，说明投资者对 AI 融资模式的审视正在深⼊。未来， AI 产业链的估值不
仅取决于算⼒需求能否持续增⻓，也将越来越取决于企业能否以可承受的融资成本，将这场资
本开⽀竞赛转化为真实、可持续的现⾦流。

### Page 5

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng 
hơn; trong khi triển vọng nhóm 
khách hàng công nghiệp bị ảnh

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 
785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025 
giúp sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế 
quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống 
của Việt Nam.

UBND TP.HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng 
mạnh từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh 
trong thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự 
án của doanh nghiệp ở mảng này sẽ vận hành hiệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và 
lợi nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với 
BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với 
giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh 
 Biwase là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực cung cấp nước sạch với 
thị phần đạt 100% tại Bình Dương. 
Ngoài ra, công ty còn mở rộng sang 
lĩnh vực đầu tư nhà máy xử lý nước và 
chất thải rắn nhằm hoàn thiện hệ 
sinh thái dịch vụ hạ tầng môi trường 
tại khu vực. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Nắng nóng thúc đẩy tiêu thụ nước sạch khối dân cư, đem về tổng doanh thu cấp nước tại Bình Dương và 
Bình Phước năm 2026/2027 đạt 2,953/3,192 tỷ VND (6.8%/7.4%). 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Nhà máy nước Nhị Thành giai đoạn 3 đi vào hoạt động giúp tổng doanh thu nhà máy đạt lần lượt 213/247 tỷ 
VND (+16%/16% YoY) trong 2026/2027 và duy trì tăng trưởng trung bình 15% đến 2030. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước: 
tại đây 
BWE hưởng lợi từ khung giá xử lý rác thải được phê duyệt, giúp doanh thu đạt 1,069/1,234 tỷ VND trong 
2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số 
 Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 
325 tỷ VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ). Mảng cung cấp nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp 
tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% 
QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025. Kinh doanh tăng trưởng 
tích cực là nhờ giá nước đầu vào mua từ TDM thấp hơn giúp tăng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp, cùng với không phát sinh lỗ tỷ giá như cùng kỳ năm ngoái.

### Page 4

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh 
được thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng 
nóng hơn; trong khi triển vọng 
nhóm khách hàng công nghiệp bị 
ảnh hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan 
 Dự báo đồng thuận xác suất xảy ra El Nino ở mức xấp xỉ 100% từ 2H2026 kéo 
dài đến tháng đầu 2027 cho thấy cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt 
nắng nóng sẽ gay gắt và kéo dài hơn so với các năm trước. Đây là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY 
(+2.6/1.1 ppts). Trái lại, sau khi section 301 được công bố và có hiệu lực thay 
thế section 122, tình hình thuế quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho 
các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống của Việt Nam. Cụ thể, Section 301 không tự 
hết hạn và có nền tảng pháp lý chắc chắn (ít nhất là trong nhiệm kỳ của 
Trump); đồng thời áp dụng hạn ngạch thuế quan cho một lượng hàng dệt may 
nhất định của một số nước không có Việt Nam, khiến bối cảnh bất lợi cho các 
ngành sản xuất truyền thống. Tuy nhiên, với việc Bình Dương đang tập trung 
đầu tư hạ tầng khu công nghiệp hiện đại nhằm thu hút dòng vốn FDI vào các 
lĩnh vực công nghệ cao, sản lượng nước sạch sẽ được duy trì khi tiêu thụ khối 
sản xuất truyền thống được bù đắp bởi nhóm công nghệ cao. Theo đó, trong 
năm 2026, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng nhẹ dự báo sản lượng nước sạch tại Bình 
Dương & Bình Phước đạt 224.9 triệu m3 (+6.8% YoY), với mức tăng của nhóm 
sản xuất/sinh hoạt/hành chính lần lượt là 5.8%/7.5%/11% YoY.

Biểu đồ 4. Xác suất tăng dần của EL Nino

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV tổng hợp

Mảng cấp nước: Tiến độ điều chỉnh 
giá bán nước gián đoạn do môi 
trường lạm phát leo cao 
 Do tác động của cuộc chiến Mỹ - Iran gây ra gián đoạn dòng chảy vận tải dầu 
tại eo biển Hormuz, giá nhiên liệu trong nước đã tăng mạnh từ đầu năm (Dầu 
Diesel - +64.3%, E5 RON 92 – +40%) và khiến cho tình hình lạm phát trong nước 
diễn biến tiêu cực: CPI bình quân 8 tháng năm 2026 tăng 4.5% với chỉ số giá 
nhóm nhà ở, điện nước, chất đốt và vật liệu xây dựng tăng cao nhất với 6.7% và 
giao thông tăng 5.4%. Trong bối cảnh đó, chúng tôi cho rằng tiến độ điều 
chỉnh giá bán nước tại các thị trường của BWE có khả năng bị đẩy lùi sang 
2027-2028 hoặc cho đến khi tình hình giá nhiên liệu ổn định trở lại. Trong dài 
hạn, khả năng đàm phán giá nước thành công là khá cao bởi: i) Thông tư 
145/2025/TT-BTC có hiệu lực từ 2026 là căn cứ để UBND quy định tỷ suất lợi 
nhuận định mức các dự án nhà máy nước; ii) BWE và cổ đông lớn là TDM dự 
kiến đầu tư và mở rộng công suất cung cấp – phân phối nước tại các vùng hẻo 
lánh nên có khả năng tăng áp lực đàm phán tăng giá nước tại Bình Dương. 
Trong kịch bản thận trọng, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng giá nước sạch trung bình khu

### Page 5
